    .registrationPopupWrapper {

<script>
    $(function () {
        $(".registrationPopupWrapper").css("width", $(window).width());
        $(".registrationPopupWrapper").css("height", $(document).height());

        $('.registrationPopupWrapper').click(function () {
            $('.registrationPopupWrapper').hide();
        });

        $(window).on('resize', function () {
            $(".registrationPopupWrapper").css("width", $(window).width());
            $(".registrationPopupWrapper").css("height", $(document).height());
        });

        var forceClose = false;

        $('#submit-button').click(function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            $.ajax({
                url: "/login/isauth",
                success: function (response) {
                    if (response == 0) {
                        if (!forceClose) {
                            $('.registrationPopupWrapper').show();
                        }
                        $("html, body").animate({
                            scrollTop: $('html').offset().top
                        }, 400);
                    } else {
                        window.parent.submitForm();
                    }
                },
                fail: function(xhr, textStatus, errorThrown){
                    console.log(xhr);
                    console.log(errorThrown);
                    console.log(textStatus);
                },
                error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                    var err = eval("(" + xhr.responseText + ")");
                    console.log(err.Message);
                }
            });
        });

        $('.loginClass').click(function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (!forceClose) {
                $('.registrationPopupWrapper').show();
            }
            $("html, body").animate({
                scrollTop: $('html').offset().top
            }, 400);
        });

        $('.form-inner-wrapper > form').on('submit', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            forceClose = true;
            $('.registrationPopupWrapper').hide();
            this.submit();
        });

    });


    function submitForm() {
        $(function () {
            $('.form-inner-wrapper > form').submit();
        });
    }
</script>
